<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      date: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">BASE_DAY</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">floor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">rand</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 365</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 86_400_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Virtualized data table: 10k+ rows render only what is on screen. Headers sort</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * on click (asc → desc → off), drag to reorder (Alt+←/→ from the keyboard) and</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
